Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the potentials object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 231. The moduli space has dimension at most 929 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 231 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the potentials structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 929. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
